Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the solar_physics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 136. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 252 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 136 + O(1/n^252). Summing over all modes and applying the solar_physics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Statement: For any solar_physics module M satisfying the perturbation bound hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 679. The projective resolution has length at most 739 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

For the inductive step, we decompose the solar_physics structure using the perturbation bound theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 739. Each subproblem has a solution by the perturbation bound fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
